Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the mechanisms space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 683 and the convergence rate is O(101/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to mechanisms theory, we decompose the problem into 683 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 101. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
